If deaths occur within 30 days of each other, they are treated as simultaneous for claim purposes, which triggers immediate payout.
The requirement to submit proof of both deaths for claim processing is a procedural follow-up to the policy's death benefit payout rules.

Logical Conclusions:
Payment is made within 30 days after complete documentation is received, which includes proof of both deaths.
